<action>
**FileEditAction**
Path: [/workspace/app-bvo9cuao8lq9/src/hooks/useBanStatus.ts]
Thought: Create a shared ban status hook:
Command: create
Created File with Text:
```
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '@/db/supabase';
import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';

export function useBanStatus() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [isBanned, setIsBanned] = useState(false);
  const [bannedUntil, setBannedUntil] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) { setIsBanned(false); setBannedUntil(null); return; }
    supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .select('banned_until')
      .eq('id', user.id)
      .maybeSingle()
      .then(({ data }) => {
        if (data?.banned_until && new Date(data.banned_until) > new Date()) {
          setIsBanned(true);
          setBannedUntil(data.banned_until);
        } else {
          setIsBanned(false);
          setBannedUntil(null);
        }
      });
  }, [user]);

  return { isBanned, bannedUntil };
}

```

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 关键词过滤管理面板
interface KeywordRow {

  const [keywords, setKeywords] = useState<KeywordRow[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [listType, setListType] = useState<'blacklist' | 'whitelist'>('blacklist');
  const [newWord, setNewWord] = useState('');
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState<'all' | 'blacklist' | 'whitelist'>('all');
  const fileInputRef = useCallback((node: HTMLInputElement | null) => { if (node) node.value = ''; }, []);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const { data } = await supabase.from('keyword_filters').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    setKeywords((data || []) as KeywordRow[]);
    setLoading(false);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const handleAdd = async () => {
    const word = newWord.trim();
    if (!word || !user) return;
    // 批量支持：逗号/换行分隔
    const words = word.split(/[,，\n]+/).map(w => w.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    setAdding(true);
    let success = 0;
    for (const w of words) {
      const { error } = await supabase.from('keyword_filters').insert({ keyword: w, list_type: listType, created_by: user.id });
      if (!error) success++;
    }
    setAdding(false);
    setNewWord('');
    if (success > 0) { toast.success(`成功添加 ${success} 个关键词`); load(); }
    else toast.error('添加失败，可能已存在');
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.from('keyword_filters').delete().eq('id', id);
    if (error) { toast.error('删除失败'); return; }
    setKeywords(prev => prev.filter(k => k.id !== id));
    toast.success('已删除');
  };

  const handleFileUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file || !user) return;
    const text = await file.text();
    // 支持 CSV（第一列关键词，可选第二列类型）或纯文本每行一个
    const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).filter(l => l.trim());
    const entries: { keyword: string; list_type: 'blacklist' | 'whitelist' }[] = [];
    for (const line of lines) {
      const parts = line.split(',');
      const kw = parts[0]?.trim();
      if (!kw || kw.toLowerCase() === 'keyword') continue; // 跳过标题行
      const lt = (parts[1]?.trim().toLowerCase() as 'blacklist' | 'whitelist') || listType;
      if (kw) entries.push({ keyword: kw, list_type: ['blacklist','whitelist'].includes(lt) ? lt : listType });
    }
    if (!entries.length) { toast.error('文件中未找到有效关键词'); return; }
    let success = 0;
    for (const entry of entries) {
      const { error } = await supabase.from('keyword_filters').insert({ ...entry, created_by: user.id });
      if (!error) success++;
    }
    toast.success(`文件导入完成，成功添加 ${success} / ${entries.length} 个关键词`);
    load();
  };

  const displayed = keywords.filter(k => filterType === 'all' || k.list_type === filterType);
  const blackCount = keywords.filter(k => k.list_type === 'blacklist').length;
  const whiteCount = keywords.filter(k => k.list_type === 'whitelist').length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* 统计卡片 */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
        <Card className="border-[#E5E6EB]">
          <CardContent className="pt-4 pb-3 px-4 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-red-50 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <Tag className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">黑名单关键词</p>
              <p className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">{blackCount}</p>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card className="border-[#E5E6EB]">
          <CardContent className="pt-4 pb-3 px-4 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-green-50 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <Tag className="w-4 h-4 text-green-500" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">白名单关键词</p>
              <p className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">{whiteCount}</p>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card className="border-[#E5E6EB] col-span-2 md:col-span-1">
          <CardContent className="pt-4 pb-3 px-4 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-blue-50 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <Filter className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-500" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">关键词总数</p>
              <p className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">{keywords.length}</p>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* 添加关键词区域 */}
      <Card className="border-[#E5E6EB]">
        <CardHeader className="pb-3">
          <CardTitle className="text-base flex items-center gap-2">
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4 text-[#165DFF]" />添加关键词
          </CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-2">
            <div className="flex gap-2 flex-1">
              <Textarea
                value={newWord}
                onChange={e => setNewWord(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="输入关键词（多个关键词用逗号或换行分隔）"
                rows={2}
                className="flex-1 resize-none text-sm"
              />
              <Select value={listType} onValueChange={v => setListType(v as 'blacklist' | 'whitelist')}>
                <SelectTrigger className="h-auto w-28 shrink-0 self-start mt-0.5">
                  <SelectValue />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="blacklist">🚫 黑名单</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="whitelist">✅ 白名单</SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            <Button onClick={handleAdd} disabled={adding || !newWord.trim()}
              className="bg-[#165DFF] hover:bg-[#165DFF]/90 text-white gap-1.5 self-start">
              {adding ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> : <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />}
              添加
            </Button>
          </div>

          {/* 文件上传 */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1 border-t border-dashed border-[#E5E6EB]">
            <Upload className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-400 shrink-0" />
            <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 flex-1">
              支持上传 TXT/CSV 文件批量导入（CSV格式：关键词,类型；TXT每行一个关键词）
            </span>
            <label className="cursor-pointer">
              <input ref={fileInputRef} type="file" accept=".txt,.csv" className="hidden" onChange={handleFileUpload} />
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-lg border border-[#E5E6EB] bg-gray-50 hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors text-gray-600">
                <Upload className="w-3 h-3" />选择文件
              </span>
            </label>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* 关键词列表 */}
      <Card className="border-[#E5E6EB]">
        <CardHeader className="pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-wrap">
            <CardTitle className="text-base flex items-center gap-2">
              <Filter className="w-4 h-4 text-[#165DFF]" />关键词列表
            </CardTitle>
            <div className="flex gap-1">
              {(['all','blacklist','whitelist'] as const).map(t => (
                <button key={t} onClick={() => setFilterType(t)}
                  className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs transition-colors ${filterType === t ? 'bg-[#165DFF] text-white' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-200'}`}>
                  {t === 'all' ? '全部' : t === 'blacklist' ? '🚫 黑名单' : '✅ 白名单'}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-8 text-gray-400">
              <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin mr-2" />加载中…
            </div>
          ) : displayed.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-400 text-sm">
              <Filter className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto mb-2 opacity-30" />暂无关键词
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto">
              {displayed.map(k => (
                <span key={k.id} className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 pl-2.5 pr-1.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium ${
                  k.list_type === 'blacklist'
                    ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200'
                    : 'bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-200'
                }`}>
                  {k.keyword}
                  <button onClick={() => handleDelete(k.id)}
                    className="ml-0.5 w-4 h-4 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-black/10 transition-colors">
                    <XIcon className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
                  </button>
                </span>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* 使用说明 */}
      <Card className="border-[#E5E6EB] bg-blue-50/50">
        <CardContent className="pt-4 pb-3 px-4">
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-blue-700 mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />使用说明
          </p>
          <ul className="text-xs text-blue-600 space-y-1 list-disc list-inside">
            <li>黑名单：含有该关键词的内容提交时将被拦截并提示用户修改</li>
            <li>白名单：该关键词不会被屏蔽（优先级高于黑名单）</li>
            <li>检测范围：论坛发帖、论坛回帖、小组消息、用户资料修改</li>
            <li>关键词匹配不区分大小写，支持模糊匹配</li>
          </ul>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

<observation>
[Read from /workspace/app-bvo9cuao8lq9/src/components/ContactAdminWidget.tsx is successful.]
Here's the result of running `cat -n` on the file: /workspace/app-bvo9cuao8lq9/src/components/ContactAdminWidget.tsx:
     1 import React, { useState } from 'react';
     2 import { MessageCircle, X, Send, ChevronDown, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
     3 import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
     4 import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
     5 import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
     6 import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
     7 import { supabase } from '@/db/supabase';
     8 import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';
     9 import { toast } from 'sonner';
    10 import LoginPromptModal from '@/components/LoginPromptModal';
    11 
    12 export default function ContactAdminWidget() {
    13   const { user } = useAuth();
    14   const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    15   const [subject, setSubject] = useState('');
    16   const [content, setContent] = useState('');
    17   const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);
    18   const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);
    19   const [loginPrompt, setLoginPrompt] = useState(false);
    20 
    21   const handleSend = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    22     e.preventDefault();
    23     if (!user) { setLoginPrompt(true); return; }
    24     if (!subject.trim() || !content.trim()) { toast.error('请填写主题和内容'); return; }
    25 
    26     setSending(true);
    27     const { data: inserted, error } = await supabase.from('contact_messages').insert({
    28       user_id: user.id,
    29       subject: subject.trim(),
    30       content: content.trim(),
    31     }).select('id').maybeSingle();
    32     setSending(false);
    33 
    34     if (error) { toast.error('发送失败，请稍后重试'); return; }
    35 
    36     // 触发邮件通知给管理员
    37     supabase.functions.invoke('notify-admin', {
    38       body: {
    39         message_id: inserted?.id,
    40         sender_name: user.email || user.id,
    41         subject: subject.trim(),
    42         content: content.trim(),
    43       },
    44     }).catch(() => {/* 通知失败不影响用户体验 */});
    45 
    46     setSent(true);
    47     setSubject('');
    48     setContent('');
    49     toast.success('消息已发送，管理员将尽快回复');
    50     setTimeout(() => { setSent(false); setOpen(false); }, 2500);
    51   };
    52 
    53   const handleOpen = () => {
    54     if (!user) { setLoginPrompt(true); return; }
    55     setOpen(v => !v);
    56   };
    57 
    58   return (
    59     <>
    60       <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex flex-col items-end gap-3">
    61         {/* 展开面板 */}
    62         {open && (
    63           <div className="w-80 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-[#E5E6EB] overflow-hidden animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 duration-200">
    64             {/* 头部 */}
    65             <div className="bg-[#165DFF] px-4 py-3 flex items-center justify-between">
    66               <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
    67                 <MessageCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
    68                 <span className="text-white text-sm font-medium">联系管理员</span>
    69               </div>
    70               <button onClick={() => setOpen(false)} className="text-white/80 hover:text-white transition-colors">
    71                 <X className="w-4 h-4" />
    72               </button>
    73             </div>
    74 
    75             {/* 内容 */}
    76             {sent ? (

**FileEditAction**
Path: [/workspace/app-bvo9cuao8lq9/src/hooks/useKeywordFilter.ts]
Thought: **Task 5 — 关键词检测客户端逻辑**

Create a shared hook that fetches the blacklist and exposes a check function:
Command: create
Created File with Text:
```
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '@/db/supabase';
import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';

let _cache: { blacklist: string[]; whitelist: string[]; ts: number } | null = null;
const CACHE_TTL = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5分钟缓存

async function fetchKeywords() {
  if (_cache && Date.now() - _cache.ts < CACHE_TTL) return _cache;
  const { data } = await supabase.from('keyword_filters').select('keyword,list_type');
  const blacklist: string[] = [];
  const whitelist: string[] = [];
  for (const row of data || []) {
    if (row.list_type === 'blacklist') blacklist.push(row.keyword.toLowerCase());
    else whitelist.push(row.keyword.toLowerCase());
  }
  _cache = { blacklist, whitelist, ts: Date.now() };
  return _cache;
}

export function useKeywordFilter() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    fetchKeywords().then(() => setReady(true)).catch(() => setReady(true));
  }, [user]);

  // 检查文本是否包含黑名单关键词（白名单优先）
  // 返回命中的关键词，若为空数组则通过
  const checkContent = useCallback(async (text: string): Promise<string[]> => {
    if (!text.trim()) return [];
    const kws = await fetchKeywords();
    const lower = text.toLowerCase();
    const hits: string[] = [];
    for (const bw of kws.blacklist) {
      if (lower.includes(bw)) {
        // 白名单优先：若命中白名单则跳过
        const inWhite = kws.whitelist.some(ww => lower.includes(ww));
        if (!inWhite) hits.push(bw);
      }
    }
    return hits;
  }, []);

  return { checkContent, ready };
}

```

   459         if (data?.banned_until) setIsBanned(new Date(data.banned_until) > new Date());
   460       });
   461     }
   462 
   463     const channel = supabase.channel(`forum-post-${post.id}`)
   464       .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'forum_replies', filter: `post_id=eq.${post.id}` },
   465         async (payload) => {
   466           const r = payload.new as ForumReply;
   467           if (r.is_deleted) return;
   468           const { data: p } = await supabase.from('public_profiles').select('full_name').eq('id', r.author_id).maybeSingle();
   469           r.author_name = p?.full_name || '用户';
   470           setReplies(prev => prev.some(x => x.id === r.id) ? prev : [...prev, r]);
   471         })
   472       .subscribe();
   473     return () => { supabase.removeChannel(channel); };
   474   }, [post.id, post.views, user]);
   475 
   476   const handleReply = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   477     e.preventDefault();
   478     if (!user) { setLoginPrompt(true); return; }
   479     if (isBanned) { toast.error('你的账号已被封禁，暂时无法回复'); return; }
   480     if (!replyText.trim()) { toast.error('回复内容不能为空'); return; }
   481     setSubmitting(true);
   482     const { error } = await supabase.from('forum_replies').insert({
   483       post_id: post.id, author_id: user.id, content: replyText.trim(),
   484     });
   485     setSubmitting(false);
   486     if (error) { toast.error('回复失败'); return; }
   487     setReplyText('');
   488     toast.success('回复成功');
   489   };
   490 
   491   const floorLabel = (idx: number) => idx === 0 ? '楼主' : `${idx + 1}楼`;
   492 
   493   return (

     6 """

    12

    14 from database import engine, Base
    15

    36 
    37 基于 **FastAPI + SQLAlchemy + MySQL** 构建的后端微服务，
    38 提供用户认证、课程管理、任务提醒、打卡计划、邮件发送等完整功能。
    39 
    40 ### 认证方式
    41 所有需要登录的接口使用 **Bearer Token** 认证。
    42 先调用 `/auth/login` 获取 Token，然后在请求头中携带：
    43 ```
    44 Authorization: Bearer <your_token>
    45 ```
    46 
    47 ### 数据库
    48 使用 **MySQL** 存储所有业务数据，通过 SQLAlchemy ORM 操作。
    49 
    50 ### 邮件服务
    51 通过 **163 邮箱 SMTP** 发送 HTML 格式邮件，
    52 需要在 `.env` 文件中配置 `EMAIL_PASS`（163 邮箱授权码）。
    53     """,
    54     version="1.0.0",
    55     docs_url="/docs",       # Swagger UI 文档地址
    56     redoc_url="/redoc",     # ReDoc 文档地址
    57 )

    60 # 允许前端 React 应用（通常运行在 localhost:5173 或其他端口）访问本服务
    61 app.add_middleware(

    63     allow_origins=[
    64         "http://localhost:5173",   # Vite 开发服务器默认端口
    65         "http://localhost:3000",   # 备用端口

    71     allow_methods=["*"],           # 允许所有 HTTP 方法

    73 )

    82

    87     """

    89     1. 自动创建数据库中尚不存在的表（已存在的表不会被修改）

Here's the result of running `cat -n` on the file: /workspace/app-bvo9cuao8lq9/python-backend/routers/features.py:
     1 """
     2 ============================================================
     3 邮件、报告、建议路由模块
     4 提供邮件发送、学习报告生成、智能建议接口
     5 ============================================================
     6 """
     7 
     8 import logging
     9 from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
    10 from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    11 from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr, Field
    12 from typing import Optional
    13 
    14 from database import get_db
    15 from models.orm_models import User
    16 from routers.auth import get_current_user
    17 from services.email_service import email_service
    18 from services.report_service import report_service
    19 from services.advice_service import advice_service
    20 
    21 logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22 
    23 router = APIRouter(tags=["邮件与智能功能"])
    24 
    25 
    26 # ── 邮件接口 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    27 
    28 class EmailSendRequest(BaseModel):
    29     """手动发送邮件请求体"""
    30     to:      EmailStr = Field(..., description="收件人邮箱")
    31     subject: str      = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=100)
    32     html:    str      = Field(..., min_length=1)
    33 
    34 
    35 @router.post("/email/send", tags=["邮件发送"], summary="手动发送邮件")
    36 def send_email(
    37     req: EmailSendRequest,
    38     current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    39     db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    40 ):
    41     """
    42     手动发送一封邮件（管理员或高级功能使用）
    43 
    44     邮件通过 163 SMTP 服务发送，并记录到 email_logs 表
    45     """
    46     result = email_service.send_email(
    47         to_email=req.to,
    48         subject=req.subject,
    49         html_content=req.html,
    50         db=db,
    51         user_id=current_user.id,
    52     )
    53     if not result["success"]:

    67     task_status: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="按状态筛选：pending/done/expired"),
    68     course_id: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="按课程筛选"),
    69     current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    70     db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    71 ):
    72     """获取当前用户的任务列表，支持多条件筛选"""
    73     query = db.query(Task).filter(Task.user_id == current_user.id)
    74 
    75     if task_type:
    76         query = query.filter(Task.type == task_type)
    77     if task_status:
    78         query = query.filter(Task.status == task_status)
    79     if course_id:
    80         query = query.filter(Task.course_id == course_id)
    81 
    82     # 按截止时间升序排列（最紧急的在前）
    83     tasks = query.order_by(Task.due_date.asc()).all()
    84 
    85     # 自动更新已过期任务状态
    86     _auto_expire_tasks(db, tasks)
    87 
    88     return [_task_to_response(t) for t in tasks]

   144         setattr(task, field, value)

   163 # ── 内部工具函数 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   164 
   165 def _get_user_task(db: Session, task_id: str, user_id: str) -> Task:
   166     """查询任务，不存在或不属于当前用户则抛出 404"""
   167     task = db.query(Task).filter(Task.id == task_id, Task.user_id == user_id).first()
   168     if not task:
   169         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="任务不存在")
   170     return task
   171 
   172 
   173 def _calc_urgency(task: Task) -> tuple:
   174     """
   175     计算任务紧急程度和剩余小时数
   176 
   177     Returns:
   178         (urgency: str, hours_left: float | None)
   179         urgency: "urgent"（24小时内）/ "warning"（7天内）/ "normal"（7天以上）/ "done" / "expired"
   180     """
   181     if task.status == TaskStatus.DONE:
   182         return "done", None
   183 
   184     now = datetime.utcnow()
   185     # 将数据库中的 datetime 和 now 统一转换进行比较
   186     due = task.due_date
   187     if due.tzinfo is not None:
   188         from datetime import timezone
   189         now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   190 
   191     hours_left = (due - now).total_seconds() / 3600
   192 
   193     if hours_left < 0:
   194         return "expired", 0.0
   195     elif hours_left <= 24:
   196         return "urgent", round(hours_left, 1)     # 24 小时内：红色紧急
   197     elif hours_left <= 168:                        # 168 = 7 * 24
   198         return "warning", round(hours_left, 1)    # 7 天内：黄色警告
   199     else:
   200         return "normal", round(hours_left, 1)     # 7 天以上：正常
   201 
   202 
   203 def _auto_expire_tasks(db: Session, tasks: list):
   204     """
   205     批量检查并更新已过期任务的状态
   206     避免前端显示过期任务仍为"待处理"
   207     """
   208     now = datetime.utcnow()
   209     updated = False
   210     for task in tasks:
   211         if task.status in (TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.IN_PROGRESS):
   212             due = task.due_date
   213             if due.tzinfo is None and due < now:
   214                 task.status = TaskStatus.EXPIRED
   215                 updated = True
   216     if updated:
   217         db.commit()
   218 
   219 
   220 def _task_to_response(t: Task) -> TaskResponse:
   221     """ORM 对象转响应体，附加计算字段"""
   222     urgency, hours_left = _calc_urgency(t)
   223     return TaskResponse(
   224         id=t.id, user_id=t.user_id, course_id=t.course_id,
   225         course_name=t.course_name, name=t.name, type=t.type.value,
   226         due_date=t.due_date.isoformat() if t.due_date else "",
   227         submit_method=t.submit_method, weight=t.weight, notes=t.notes,
   228         status=t.status.value, urgency=urgency, hours_left=hours_left,
   229         reminder_3d=t.reminder_3d, reminder_1d=t.reminder_1d, reminder_1h=t.reminder_1h,
   230         created_at=t.created_at.isoformat() if t.created_at else "",
   231     )
   232

     2 ============================================================
     3 用户认证路由模块
     4 提供注册、登录、获取当前用户信息等接口
     5 ============================================================
     6 """
     7 
     8 import logging
     9 from datetime import datetime, timedelta
    10 from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
    11 from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer, OAuth2PasswordRequestForm
    12 from jose import JWTError, jwt                  # JWT 生成与验证
    13 from passlib.context import CryptContext        # 密码哈希
    14 from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    15 from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, EmailStr
    16 from typing import Optional
    17 
    18 from database import get_db
    19 from models.orm_models import User, UserRole
    20 from config import settings
    21 
    22 logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23 
    24 # ── 路由实例 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    25 # prefix="/auth" 表示所有路由路径以 /auth 开头
    26 # tags=["认证"] 用于 Swagger 文档分组
    27 router = APIRouter(prefix="/auth", tags=["认证"])
    28 
    29 # ── 密码哈希工具 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    30 # bcrypt 是目前最安全的密码哈希算法之一，自带盐值
    31 pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")
    32 
    33 # ── OAuth2 Bearer Token 配置 ──────────────────────────────────
    34 # tokenUrl 指向登录接口路径
    35 oauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl="/auth/login")
    36 
    37 
    38 # ── 工具函数 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    39 
    40 def hash_password(plain_password: str) -> str:
    41     """将明文密码加密为 bcrypt 哈希值"""
    42     return pwd_context.hash(plain_password)
    43 
    44 
    45 def verify_password(plain_password: str, hashed_password: str) -> bool:
    46     """验证明文密码与哈希值是否匹配"""
    47     return pwd_context.verify(plain_password, hashed_password)
    48 
    49 
    50 def create_access_token(data: dict) -> str:
    51     """
    52     生成 JWT Access Token
    53 
    54     Args:
    55         data: 要编码到 Token 中的数据（通常包含 user_id）
    56 
    57     Returns:
    58         str: 签名后的 JWT 字符串
    59     """
    60     to_encode = data.copy()
    61     # 设置 Token 过期时间
    62     expire = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=settings.access_token_expire_minutes)
    63     to_encode.update({"exp": expire})
    64     # 使用 HS256 算法和密钥签名
    65     return jwt.encode(to_encode, settings.secret_key, algorithm=settings.algorithm)
    66 
    67 
    68 def get_current_user(
    69     token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme),
    70     db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    71 ) -> User:
    72     """
    73     FastAPI 依赖项：从 Bearer Token 中解析并返回当前登录用户
    74 
    75     用法：在需要认证的路由函数中加入 current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
    76     """
    77     credentials_exception = HTTPException(
    78         status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
    79         detail="登录已过期或 Token 无效，请重新登录",
    80         headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
    81     )
    82     try:
    83         # 解码 JWT Token
    84         payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.secret_key, algorithms=[settings.algorithm])
    85         user_id: str = payload.get("sub")
    86         if user_id is None:
    87             raise credentials_exception
    88     except JWTError:
    89         raise credentials_exception
    90 
    91     # 从数据库查询用户
    92     user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()
    93     if user is None or not user.is_active:
    94         raise credentials_exception
    95 
    96     return user
    97 
    98

   104     password:  str   = Field(..., min_length=6, max_length=50, description="密码（至少6位）")
   105     full_name: Optional[str]      = Field(None, description="真实姓名（可选）")
   106     email:     Optional[EmailStr] = Field(None, description="邮箱（可选，用于接收提醒）")

   137     full_name:  Optional[str]      = None
   138     school:     Optional[str]      = None
   139     major:      Optional[str]      = None

   145 # ── API 接口 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   146 
   147 @router.post("/register", summary="用户注册")
   148 def register(req: RegisterRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
   149     """
   150     注册新用户
   151 
   152     - 检查用户名是否已被占用
   153     - 密码使用 bcrypt 加密后存储
   154     - 注册成功后返回 JWT Token（可直接登录）
   155     """
   156     # 检查用户名是否已存在
   157     if db.query(User).filter(User.username == req.username).first():
   158         raise HTTPException(
   159             status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
   160             detail=f"用户名 '{req.username}' 已被占用，请换一个"
   161         )
   162 
   163     # 创建新用户，密码哈希存储
   164     new_user = User(
   165         username=req.username,
   166         password_hash=hash_password(req.password),
   167         full_name=req.full_name,
   168         email=req.email,
   169     )
   170     db.add(new_user)
   171     db.commit()
   172     db.refresh(new_user)   # 刷新获取数据库生成的字段（如 created_at）
   173 
   174     logger.info(f"新用户注册成功：{req.username}")
   175 
   176     # 注册后自动生成 Token
   177     token = create_access_token({"sub": new_user.id})
   178     return {
   179         "message":      "注册成功",
   180         "access_token": token,
   181         "token_type":   "bearer",
   182         "user_id":      new_user.id,
   183         "username":     new_user.username,
   184     }
   185 
   186 
   187 @router.post("/login", response_model=LoginResponse, summary="用户登录")
   188 def login(
   189     form_data: OAuth2PasswordRequestForm = Depends(),
   190     db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   191 ):
   192     """
   193     用户登录（支持表单提交，兼容 OAuth2 标准）
   194 
   195     - 验证用户名和密码
   196     - 登录成功返回 JWT Token
   197     - Token 默认有效期 7 天
   198     """
   199     # 查询用户
   200     user = db.query(User).filter(User.username == form_data.username).first()
   201 
   202     # 用户不存在或密码错误（统一提示，避免泄露用户是否存在的信息）
   203     if not user or not verify_password(form_data.password, user.password_hash):
   204         raise HTTPException(
   205             status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
   206             detail="用户名或密码错误",
   207             headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
   208         )
   209 
   210     if not user.is_active:
   211         raise HTTPException(
   212             status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
   213             detail="账号已被禁用，请联系管理员"
   214         )
   215 
   216     token = create_access_token({"sub": user.id})
   217     logger.info(f"用户登录成功：{user.username}")
   218 
   219     return LoginResponse(
   220         access_token=token,
   221         user_id=user.id,
   222         username=user.username,
   223         role=user.role.value,
   224     )
   225 
   226 
   227 @router.get("/me", response_model=UserProfileResponse, summary="获取当前用户信息")
   228 def get_me(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
   229     """获取当前登录用户的完整信息"""
   230     return UserProfileResponse(
   231         id=current_user.id,
   232         username=current_user.username,
   233         full_name=current_user.full_name,
   234         school=current_user.school,
   235         major=current_user.major,
   236         grade=current_user.grade,
   237         email=current_user.email,
   238         avatar_url=current_user.avatar_url,
   239         role=current_user.role.value,
   240         created_at=current_user.created_at.isoformat() if current_user.created_at else "",
   241     )
   242 
   243 
   244 @router.put("/me", summary="更新当前用户资料")
   245 def update_me(
   246     req: UpdateProfileRequest,
   247     current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   248     db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   249 ):
   250     """更新当前登录用户的个人资料（仅修改提交的字段）"""
   251     # 只更新请求中非 None 的字段
   252     update_data = req.model_dump(exclude_none=True)
   253     if not update_data:
   254         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="没有需要更新的字段")
   255 
   256     for field, value in update_data.items():
   257         setattr(current_user, field, value)
   258 
   259     db.commit()
   260     db.refresh(current_user)
   261     return {"message": "资料更新成功"}

    69 def list_courses(
    70     day_of_week: Optional[int] = None,   # 可按星期筛选
    71     current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    72     db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    73 ):
    74     """获取当前用户的所有课程，可按星期筛选"""
    75     query = db.query(Course).filter(Course.user_id == current_user.id)
    76     if day_of_week is not None:
    77         query = query.filter(Course.day_of_week == day_of_week)
    78     courses = query.order_by(Course.day_of_week, Course.start_time).all()
    79     return [_course_to_response(c) for c in courses]
    80 
    81 
    82 @router.post("/", response_model=CourseResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED, summary="添加课程")
    83 def create_course(
    84     req: CourseCreate,
    85     check_conflict: bool = True,         # 查询参数：是否检测冲突，默认开启
    86     current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    87     db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    88 ):
    89     """
    90     添加新课程
    91 
    92     - check_conflict=true（默认）：自动检测与已有课程的时间冲突
    93     - 存在冲突时返回 409 并说明冲突详情
    94     - check_conflict=false：跳过检测，强制添加
    95     """
    96     # 验证时间合法性（开始时间 < 结束时间）
    97     if req.start_time >= req.end_time:
    98         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="开始时间必须早于结束时间")
    99 
   100     if check_conflict:
   101         # 调用冲突检测服务检测新课程与已有课程的冲突
   102         result = conflict_detector.detect(
   103             db=db,
   104             user_id=current_user.id,
   105             new_course_data={
   106                 "id":          "new",
   107                 "name":        req.name,
   108                 "day_of_week": req.day_of_week,
   109                 "start_time":  req.start_time,
   110                 "end_time":    req.end_time,
   111                 "start_week":  req.start_week,
   112                 "end_week":    req.end_week,
   113             },
   114         )
   115         if result["has_conflict"]:
   116             # 返回 409 冲突状态码，前端可提示用户确认
   117             raise HTTPException(
   118                 status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
   119                 detail={
   120                     "message": "课程时间存在冲突",
   121                     "conflicts": result["conflicts"],
   122                     "hint": "若确认添加，请在请求中加 ?check_conflict=false",
   123                 },
   124             )
   125 
   126     # 创建课程记录
   127     course = Course(
   128         user_id=current_user.id,
   129         **req.model_dump(),
   130     )
   131     db.add(course)
   132     db.commit()
   133     db.refresh(course)
   134     logger.info(f"用户 {current_user.username} 添加课程：{req.name}")
   135     return _course_to_response(course)

    47 @router.get("/", summary="获取我的学习目标列表")
    48 def list_goals(
    49     current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    50     db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    51 ):
    52     """获取当前用户的所有学习目标（含连续打卡天数统计）"""
    53     goals = (
    54         db.query(Goal)
    55         .filter(Goal.user_id == current_user.id, Goal.is_active == True)
    56         .order_by(Goal.created_at.desc())
    57         .all()
    58     )
    59 
    60     result = []
    61     today_str = date.today().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    62 
    63     for goal in goals:
    64         # 统计该目标的总打卡次数
    65         total_checkins = (
    66             db.query(func.count(Checkin.id))
    67             .filter(Checkin.goal_id == goal.id)
    68             .scalar() or 0
    69         )
    70         # 今日是否已打卡
    71         today_checked = (
    72             db.query(func.count(Checkin.id))
    73             .filter(Checkin.goal_id == goal.id, Checkin.checkin_date == today_str)
    74             .scalar() or 0
    75         ) > 0
    76 
    77         result.append({
    78             "id":             goal.id,
    79             "name":           goal.name,
    80             "description":    goal.description,
    81             "period":         goal.period.value,
    82             "target_value":   goal.target_value,
    83             "unit":           goal.unit,
    84             "category":       goal.category,
    85             "total_checkins": total_checkins,
    86             "today_checked":  today_checked,
    87             "created_at":     goal.created_at.isoformat() if goal.created_at else "",
    88         })

   109 def delete_goal(
   110     goal_id: str,
   111     current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   112     db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   113 ):
   114     """软删除学习目标（标记为不活跃，不物理删除，保留打卡历史）"""
   115     goal = db.query(Goal).filter(Goal.id == goal_id, Goal.user_id == current_user.id).first()
   116     if not goal:
   117         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="目标不存在")
   118     goal.is_active = False   # 软删除，保留历史数据
   119     db.commit()
   120     return {"message": "目标已停用"}
   121 
   122 
   123 # ── 打卡接口 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   124 
   125 @router.post("/checkins", status_code=201, summary="提交打卡记录")
   126 def create_checkin(
   127     req: CheckinCreate,
   128     current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   129     db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   130 ):
   131     """
   132     提交一次打卡记录
   133 
   134     - checkin_date 不填则默认为今天
   135     - 同一目标同一天可以多次打卡（累计时长）
   136     """
   137     # 验证目标是否属于当前用户
   138     goal = db.query(Goal).filter(
   139         Goal.id == req.goal_id, Goal.user_id == current_user.id
   140     ).first()
   141     if not goal:
   142         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="学习目标不存在")
   143 
   144     # 未提供日期时默认为今天
   145     checkin_date = req.checkin_date or date.today().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
   146 
   147     checkin = Checkin(
   148         goal_id=req.goal_id,
   149         user_id=current_user.id,
   150         checkin_date=checkin_date,
   151         duration_minutes=req.duration_minutes,
   152         notes=req.notes,
   153     )
   154     db.add(checkin)
   155     db.commit()
   156     db.refresh(checkin)
   157 
   158     logger.info(
   159         f"用户 {current_user.username} 打卡：目标={goal.name}，"
   160         f"日期={checkin_date}，时长={req.duration_minutes}分钟"
   161     )
   162     return {
   163         "message":    "打卡成功！",
   164         "id":         checkin.id,
   165         "goal_name":  goal.name,
   166         "date":       checkin_date,
   167         "duration":   req.duration_minutes,
   168     }
   169 
   170 
   171 @router.get("/checkins", summary="获取打卡记录列表")
   172 def list_checkins(
   173     goal_id:    Optional[str] = Query(None, description="按目标筛选"),
   174     start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="开始日期 YYYY-MM-DD"),
   175     end_date:   Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期 YYYY-MM-DD"),
   176     current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   177     db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   178 ):
   179     """获取打卡记录，支持按目标和日期范围筛选"""
   180     query = db.query(Checkin).filter(Checkin.user_id == current_user.id)
   181 
   182     if goal_id:
   183         query = query.filter(Checkin.goal_id == goal_id)
   184     if start_date:
   185         query = query.filter(Checkin.checkin_date >= start_date)
   186     if end_date:
   187         query = query.filter(Checkin.checkin_date <= end_date)
   188 
   189     checkins = query.order_by(Checkin.checkin_date.desc(), Checkin.created_at.desc()).all()
   190 
   191     return [
   192         {
   193             "id":               c.id,
   194             "goal_id":          c.goal_id,
   195             "checkin_date":     c.checkin_date,
   196             "duration_minutes": c.duration_minutes,
   197             "notes":            c.notes,
   198             "created_at":       c.created_at.isoformat() if c.created_at else "",
   199         }
   200         for c in checkins
   201     ]
   202 
   203 
   204 @router.get("/stats/summary", summary="获取打卡统计摘要")
   205 def get_stats_summary(
   206     current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   207     db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   208 ):
   209     """
   210     获取当前用户的整体打卡统计摘要
   211     包含：总打卡次数、总学习时长、今日打卡情况
   212     """
   213     today_str = date.today().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
   214 
   215     # 总打卡次数
   216     total_checkins = (
   217         db.query(func.count(Checkin.id))
   218         .filter(Checkin.user_id == current_user.id)
   219         .scalar() or 0
   220     )
   221 
   222     # 总学习时长（分钟）
   223     total_minutes = (
   224         db.query(func.sum(Checkin.duration_minutes))
   225         .filter(Checkin.user_id == current_user.id)
   226         .scalar() or 0
   227     )
   228 
   229     # 今日打卡次数和时长
   230     today_checkins = (
   231         db.query(func.count(Checkin.id))
   232         .filter(Checkin.user_id == current_user.id, Checkin.checkin_date == today_str)
   233         .scalar() or 0
   234     )
   235     today_minutes = (
   236         db.query(func.sum(Checkin.duration_minutes))
   237         .filter(Checkin.user_id == current_user.id, Checkin.checkin_date == today_str)
   238         .scalar() or 0
   239     )
   240 
   241     return {
   242         "total_checkins":   total_checkins,
   243         "total_minutes":    total_minutes,
   244         "total_hours":      round(total_minutes / 60, 1),
   245         "today_checkins":   today_checkins,
   246         "today_minutes":    today_minutes,
   247     }
   248

   284     created_at = Column(DateTime, server_default=func.now(), comment="通知创建时间")

   289         Index("ix_notifications_user_read", "user_id", "is_read"),

     3 智能学习建议生成服务模块
     4 根据用户近期打卡数据、任务完成情况，生成个性化学习建议
     5 ============================================================
     6 """
     7 
     8 import logging
     9 from datetime import date, timedelta

    12

    14 
    15 logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    17 # 评分权重配置（总分 100 分）
    18 SCORE_WEIGHT_CHECKIN   = 40   # 打卡频率占 40 分
    19 SCORE_WEIGHT_TASK      = 35   # 任务完成率占 35 分
    20 SCORE_WEIGHT_DURATION  = 25   # 日均学习时长占 25 分
    21 
    22 
    23 class AdviceService:
    24     """
    25     智能学习建议服务
    26     基于规则引擎分析学习数据，生成针对性建议文本
    27     （不依赖外部 AI 接口，纯本地计算，适合实验环境使用）
    28     """
    29 
    30     def generate(self, db: Session, user_id: str) -> dict:
    31         """
    32         分析用户近 14 天的学习数据，生成评分和建议
    33 
    34         Args:
    35             db:       数据库会话
    36             user_id:  用户 ID
    37 
    38         Returns:
    39             dict: {
    40                 "score": int,           # 综合得分 0-100
    41                 "overall_rating": str,  # 评级：优秀/良好/一般/需加油
    42                 "suggestions": [str],   # 建议列表
    43                 "stats": dict,          # 原始统计数据（供前端展示）
    44             }
    45         """
    46         # ── 统计近 14 天数据 ─────────────────────────────────
    47         today      = date.today()
    48         start_date = today - timedelta(days=13)
    49         start_str  = start_date.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    50         end_str    = today.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    51 
    52         # 1. 打卡天数和总学习时长
    53         checkin_rows = (
    54             db.query(
    55                 Checkin.checkin_date,
    56                 func.sum(Checkin.duration_minutes).label("daily_minutes"),
    57                 func.count(Checkin.id).label("daily_count"),
    58             )
    59             .filter(
    60                 Checkin.user_id == user_id,
    61                 Checkin.checkin_date >= start_str,
    62                 Checkin.checkin_date <= end_str,
    63             )
    64             .group_by(Checkin.checkin_date)
    65             .all()
    66         )
    67 
    68         checkin_days   = len(checkin_rows)                            # 打卡天数
    69         total_days     = 14                                           # 统计区间总天数
    70         total_minutes  = sum(row.daily_minutes for row in checkin_rows)
    71         avg_minutes    = total_minutes / max(checkin_days, 1)         # 打卡日的日均时长
    72 
    73         # 2. 任务完成率
    74         all_tasks = (
    75             db.query(Task)
    76             .filter(Task.user_id == user_id)
    77             .all()
    78         )
    79         total_tasks     = len(all_tasks)
    80         completed_count = sum(1 for t in all_tasks if t.status == TaskStatus.DONE)
    81         task_rate       = completed_count / max(total_tasks, 1)       # 完成率 0~1
    82 
    83         # 3. 过期未完成的任务数（重要的负向指标）
    84         expired_tasks = (
    85             db.query(func.count(Task.id))
    86             .filter(
    87                 Task.user_id == user_id,
    88                 Task.status == TaskStatus.EXPIRED,
    89             )
    90             .scalar() or 0
    91         )
    92 
    93         # 4. 目标设置数量
    94         goal_count = (
    95             db.query(func.count(Goal.id))
    96             .filter(Goal.user_id == user_id, Goal.is_active == True)
    97             .scalar() or 0
    98         )
    99 
   100         logger.info(
   101             f"用户 {user_id} 近14天数据：打卡 {checkin_days} 天，"
   102             f"均学 {avg_minutes:.0f} 分/天，任务完成率 {task_rate:.0%}"
   103         )
   104 
   105         # ── 计算综合评分 ─────────────────────────────────────
   106         score = self._calc_score(
   107             checkin_days=checkin_days,
   108             total_days=total_days,
   109             avg_minutes=avg_minutes,
   110             task_rate=task_rate,
   111             expired_tasks=expired_tasks,
   112         )

   126         overall_rating = self._score_to_rating(score)

   130             "overall_rating": overall_rating,
   131             "suggestions":    suggestions,

   136                 "task_rate":      round(task_rate * 100),
   137                 "expired_tasks":  expired_tasks,
   138                 "goal_count":     goal_count,
   139             },
   140         }

   142     def _calc_score(
   143         self,
   144         checkin_days: int,
   145         total_days: int,
   146         avg_minutes: float,
   147         task_rate: float,
   148         expired_tasks: int,
   149     ) -> int:
   150         """
   151         计算综合学习评分（0-100）
   152 
   153         评分维度：
   154         - 打卡频率（40分）：打卡天数 / 总天数 × 40
   155         - 任务完成率（35分）：完成率 × 35
   156         - 日均学习时长（25分）：120分钟为满分基准
   157         - 过期任务扣分：每个过期任务扣 5 分，最多扣 20 分
   158         """
   159         # 打卡频率得分（0-40）
   160         checkin_score  = (checkin_days / max(total_days, 1)) * SCORE_WEIGHT_CHECKIN
   161 
   162         # 任务完成率得分（0-35）
   163         task_score     = task_rate * SCORE_WEIGHT_TASK
   164 
   165         # 日均学习时长得分（0-25）：以 120 分钟/天为满分基准
   166         duration_score = min(avg_minutes / 120, 1.0) * SCORE_WEIGHT_DURATION
   167 
   168         # 基础得分
   169         raw_score = checkin_score + task_score + duration_score
   170 
   171         # 过期任务扣分（每个扣 5 分，最多扣 20 分）
   172         penalty = min(expired_tasks * 5, 20)
   173 
   174         # 最终得分，限定在 0-100 范围内，取整
   175         final_score = max(0, min(100, int(raw_score - penalty)))
   176         return final_score
   177 
   178     def _score_to_rating(self, score: int) -> str:
   179         """根据分数返回评级文字"""
   180         if score >= 85:
   181             return "优秀 🏆"
   182         elif score >= 70:
   183             return "良好 👍"
   184         elif score >= 55:
   185             return "一般 📈"
   186         else:
   187             return "需加油 💪"
   188 
   189     def _build_suggestions(
   190         self,

   211         checkin_rate = checkin_days / max(total_days, 1)  # 打卡率

   213         # ── 建议 1：打卡频率 ──────────────────────────────────
   214         if checkin_rate < 0.3:

   218             )

   221                 f"📅 近 {total_days} 天打卡 {checkin_days} 天，坚持得不错！"
   222                 "试着做到每天打卡，保持学习节奏，避免三天打鱼两天晒网。"
   223             )
   224         else:
   225             suggestions.append(
   226                 f"🎯 太棒了！近 {total_days} 天坚持打卡 {checkin_days} 天，打卡率达 "
   227                 f"{checkin_rate:.0%}！持续保持这种自律精神。"
   228             )
   229 
   230         # ── 建议 2：学习时长 ──────────────────────────────────
   231         if avg_minutes < 30:
   232             suggestions.append(
   233                 f"⏱️ 打卡日平均学习仅 {avg_minutes:.0f} 分钟，时间稍短。"
   234                 "建议尝试「番茄工作法」：每次专注 25 分钟，休息 5 分钟，逐步延长学习时长。"
   235             )
   236         elif avg_minutes < 60:
   237             suggestions.append(
   238                 f"⏱️ 打卡日平均学习 {avg_minutes:.0f} 分钟，基础不错。"
   239                 "若能坚持到每天 60-90 分钟，学习效果会显著提升。"
   240             )
   241         elif avg_minutes < 120:
   242             suggestions.append(
   243                 f"⏱️ 打卡日平均学习 {avg_minutes:.0f} 分钟，表现良好！"
   244                 "注意劳逸结合，每学习 50 分钟建议休息 10 分钟，保护视力和注意力。"
   245             )
   246         else:
   247             suggestions.append(
   248                 f"⏱️ 打卡日平均学习 {avg_minutes:.0f} 分钟，学习投入度很高！"
   249                 "记得适当休息，保证充足睡眠，高质量的学习比单纯的长时间更重要。"
   250             )
   251 
   252         # ── 建议 3：任务完成率 ────────────────────────────────
   253         if task_rate < 0.4:
   254             suggestions.append(
   255                 f"📋 任务完成率仅 {task_rate:.0%}，有较多任务积压。"

   257             )

   265                 f"📋 任务完成率高达 {task_rate:.0%}，执行力很强！"

   267             )
   268 
   269         # ── 建议 4：过期任务专项建议 ──────────────────────────
   270         if expired_tasks > 0:
   271             suggestions.append(

   282             )
   283         elif goal_count >= 5:
   284             suggestions.append(

   286                 "目标太多有时会分散注意力，建议聚焦于最重要的 2-3 个，深入突破。"
   287             )
   288

   290         if score >= 85:

   292                 "🏆 综合评分优秀，您是学习的榜样！"

     1 """
     2 ============================================================
     3 邮件发送服务模块
     4 使用 Python 标准库 smtplib 通过 163 邮箱 SMTP 发送 HTML 邮件
     5 ============================================================
     6 """
     7 
     8 import smtplib
     9 import logging
    10 from email.mime.multipart import MIMEMultipart   # 多部分邮件容器
    11 from email.mime.text import MIMEText             # 文本/HTML 邮件部分
    12 from email.header import Header                  # 处理中文主题编码
    13 from email.utils import formataddr               # 格式化发件人 "名称 <地址>"
    14 
    15 from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    16 from config import settings
    17 from models.orm_models import EmailLog
    18 
    19 # 配置日志记录器，方便排查发送问题
    20 logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    21 
    22 
    23 class EmailService:
    24     """
    25     邮件发送服务类
    26     封装 163 SMTP 连接、HTML 邮件构建、发送日志记录等功能
    27     """
    28 
    29     # 163 邮箱 SMTP 服务器配置
    30     SMTP_HOST = "smtp.163.com"
    31     SMTP_PORT = 465          # SSL 加密端口，比 25 端口更安全稳定
    32 
    33     def send_email(
    34         self,
    35         to_email: str,
    36         subject: str,
    37         html_content: str,
    38         db: Session = None,
    39         user_id: str = None,
    40     ) -> dict:
    41         """
    42         发送一封 HTML 格式邮件
    43 
    44         Args:
    45             to_email (str):      收件人邮箱地址
    46             subject (str):       邮件主题
    47             html_content (str):  邮件 HTML 正文
    48             db (Session):        数据库会话，传入则记录发送日志（可选）
    49             user_id (str):       关联用户 ID，用于日志关联（可选）
    50 
    51         Returns:
    52             dict: {"success": True/False, "message": "说明信息"}
    53         """
    54 
    55         # 检查 163 邮箱授权码是否已配置
    56         if not settings.email_pass:
    57             msg = "163 邮箱授权码未配置，请在 .env 文件中设置 EMAIL_PASS"
    58             logger.error(msg)
    59             self._save_log(db, user_id, to_email, subject, "failed", msg)
    60             return {"success": False, "message": msg}
    61 
    62         try:
    63             # ── 步骤 1：构建邮件消息 ──────────────────────────────
    64             # MIMEMultipart("alternative") 表示邮件包含多种格式（纯文本 + HTML）
    65             # 邮件客户端会自动选择最佳格式显示
    66             msg = MIMEMultipart("alternative")
    67 
    68             # 设置发件人（使用 formataddr 处理中文名称，防止乱码）
    69             msg["From"] = formataddr(
    70                 (settings.email_from_name, settings.email_user),
    71                 charset="utf-8"
    72             )
    73             # 设置收件人
    74             msg["To"] = to_email
    75             # 设置主题（Header 处理中文编码，防止主题显示乱码）
    76             msg["Subject"] = Header(subject, "utf-8")
    77 
    78             # 将 HTML 内容附加到邮件（指定编码为 utf-8 支持中文）
    79             html_part = MIMEText(html_content, "html", "utf-8")
    80             msg.attach(html_part)
    81 
    82             # ── 步骤 2：连接 SMTP 服务器并发送 ────────────────────
    83             # 使用 SMTP_SSL 建立加密连接（SSL 在连接建立时就加密）
    84             # 与 starttls 不同，SSL 模式从一开始就是加密的，更安全
    85             with smtplib.SMTP_SSL(self.SMTP_HOST, self.SMTP_PORT) as server:
    86                 # 设置调试级别：0=不输出调试信息，1=输出 SMTP 通信过程
    87                 server.set_debuglevel(0)
    88 
    89                 # 登录：使用账号 + 授权码（不是登录密码！）
    90                 server.login(settings.email_user, settings.email_pass)
    91                 logger.info(f"SMTP 登录成功：{settings.email_user}")
    92 
    93                 # 发送邮件
    94                 server.sendmail(
    95                     from_addr=settings.email_user,
    96                     to_addrs=[to_email],
    97                     msg=msg.as_string()
    98                 )
    99                 logger.info(f"邮件发送成功 → {to_email}，主题：{subject}")
   100 
   101             # ── 步骤 3：记录成功日志 ──────────────────────────────
   102             self._save_log(db, user_id, to_email, subject, "success")
   103             return {"success": True, "message": f"邮件已成功发送至 {to_email}"}
   104 
   105         except smtplib.SMTPAuthenticationError:
   106             # 认证失败：通常是授权码错误或服务未开启
   107             err = "SMTP 认证失败：请检查 163 邮箱授权码是否正确，以及是否已开启 SMTP 服务"
   108             logger.error(err)
   109             self._save_log(db, user_id, to_email, subject, "failed", err)
   110             return {"success": False, "message": err}
   111 
   112         except smtplib.SMTPException as e:
   113             # 其他 SMTP 协议错误
   114             err = f"SMTP 发送异常：{str(e)}"
   115             logger.error(err)
   116             self._save_log(db, user_id, to_email, subject, "failed", err)
   117             return {"success": False, "message": err}
   118 
   119         except Exception as e:
   120             # 捕获所有其他异常，防止服务崩溃
   121             err = f"邮件发送失败（未知错误）：{str(e)}"
   122             logger.error(err, exc_info=True)
   123             self._save_log(db, user_id, to_email, subject, "failed", err)
   124             return {"success": False, "message": err}
   125 
   126     def _save_log(
   127         self,
   128         db: Session,
   129         user_id: str,
   130         to_email: str,
   131         subject: str,
   132         status: str,
   133         error_msg: str = None,
   134     ):
   135         """
   136         将邮件发送结果写入 email_logs 表
   137 
   138         Args:
   139             db:        数据库会话（为 None 则跳过日志记录）
   140             user_id:   关联用户 ID
   141             to_email:  收件人邮箱
   142             subject:   邮件主题
   143             status:    "success" 或 "failed"
   144             error_msg: 失败时的错误信息
   145         """
   146         if db is None:
   147             return  # 无数据库会话时跳过日志记录
   148         try:
   149             log = EmailLog(
   150                 user_id=user_id,
   151                 to_email=to_email,
   152                 subject=subject,
   153                 status=status,
   154                 error_msg=error_msg,
   155             )
   156             db.add(log)
   157             db.commit()
   158         except Exception as e:
   159             # 日志记录失败不影响主流程
   160             logger.warning(f"邮件日志写入失败：{e}")
   161             db.rollback()
   162 
   163     # ── 预定义邮件模板 ────────────────────────────────────────
   164 
   165     def build_task_reminder_html(
   166         self,
   167         username: str,
   168         task_name: str,
   169         course_name: str,
   170         due_date: str,
   171         hours_left: int,
   172     ) -> str:
   173         """
   174         构建任务截止提醒邮件的 HTML 内容
   175 
   176         Args:
   177             username:    学生姓名
   178             task_name:   任务名称

   181             hours_left:  距截止还剩多少小时

   183         Returns:
   184             str: 完整 HTML 字符串
   185         """
   186         # 根据剩余时间决定颜色和紧急度文案
   187         if hours_left <= 24:
   188             urgency_color = "#FF4D4F"   # 红色：紧急
   189             urgency_label = "⚠️ 紧急提醒"
   190         elif hours_left <= 72:

   193         else:

   195             urgency_label = "📋 任务提醒"
   196 
   197         return f"""

   199                     max-width: 520px; margin: 0 auto; background: #f5f7fa; padding: 24px;">

   201                       box-shadow: 0 2px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.08);">
   202

   205               <h2 style="color: #fff; margin: 0; font-size: 18px;">{urgency_label}</h2>
   206               <p style="color: rgba(255,255,255,0.85); margin: 4px 0 0; font-size: 13px;">
   207                 学习助手 · 大学生学习管理平台
   208               </p>
   209             </div>
   210 
   211             <!-- 主体内容 -->
   212             <div style="padding: 24px 28px;">
   213               <p style="color: #333; font-size: 15px; margin: 0 0 16px;">
   214                 亲爱的 <strong>{username}</strong>，您有一项任务即将截止：
   215               </p>

   217               <!-- 任务信息卡 -->

     1 """
     2 ============================================================
     3 课程冲突检测服务模块
     4 从 MySQL 读取课程数据，检测时间重叠的课程对
     5 ============================================================
     6 """
     7 
     8 import logging
     9 from typing import List, Optional
    10 from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    11 from models.orm_models import Course
    12 
    13 logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    14 
    15 
    16 class ConflictDetector:
    17     """
    18     课程冲突检测器
    19     两门课程冲突的判定条件：
    20       1. 同一用户
    21       2. 同一星期
    22       3. 周次范围有交集（如第1-8周 与 第5-18周 存在交集）
    23       4. 上课时间段有重叠（时间区间交叉）
    24     """
    25 
    26     def _time_to_minutes(self, time_str: str) -> int:
    27         """
    28         将 HH:MM 格式的时间转换为从 00:00 开始的分钟数
    29 
    30         例如：
    31             "08:00" → 480
    32             "09:40" → 580
    33 
    34         Args:
    35             time_str: 时间字符串，格式 HH:MM
    36 
    37         Returns:
    38             int: 分钟数
    39         """
    40         try:
    41             parts = time_str.split(":")
    42             return int(parts[0]) * 60 + int(parts[1])
    43         except (ValueError, IndexError, AttributeError):
    44             logger.warning(f"时间格式解析失败：{time_str}，将返回 0")
    45             return 0
    46 
    47     def _time_overlap(self, start_a: str, end_a: str, start_b: str, end_b: str) -> bool:
    48         """
    49         判断两个时间区间是否存在重叠
    50 
    51         两区间不重叠的条件：A 结束 <= B 开始，或 B 结束 <= A 开始
    52         取反即为重叠条件
    53 
    54         Args:
    55             start_a, end_a: 课程 A 的时间区间
    56             start_b, end_b: 课程 B 的时间区间
    57 
    58         Returns:
    59             bool: True 表示时间重叠（冲突）
    60         """
    61         sa = self._time_to_minutes(start_a)
    62         ea = self._time_to_minutes(end_a)
    63         sb = self._time_to_minutes(start_b)
    64         eb = self._time_to_minutes(end_b)
    65 
    66         # 区间重叠：A 开始 < B 结束，且 B 开始 < A 结束
    67         return sa < eb and sb < ea
    68 
    69     def _week_overlap(
    70         self,
    71         sw_a: Optional[int], ew_a: Optional[int],
    72         sw_b: Optional[int], ew_b: Optional[int],
    73     ) -> bool:
    74         """
    75         判断两门课程的周次范围是否有交集
    76 
    77         若任一课程未设置周次，视为全学期（第1-25周），一定有交集
    78 
    79         Args:
    80             sw_a, ew_a: 课程 A 的起始/结束周次
    81             sw_b, ew_b: 课程 B 的起始/结束周次
    82 
    83         Returns:
    84             bool: True 表示周次有交集
    85         """
    86         # 未设置周次时默认为全学期
    87         sw_a = sw_a or 1
    88         ew_a = ew_a or 25
    89         sw_b = sw_b or 1
    90         ew_b = ew_b or 25
    91 
    92         # 周次区间重叠判断与时间区间相同
    93         return sw_a <= ew_b and sw_b <= ew_a
    94 
    95     def detect(
    96         self,
    97         db: Session,
    98         user_id: str,
    99         new_course_data: dict = None,
   100     ) -> dict:
   101         """
   102         执行课程冲突检测
   103 
   104         使用场景：
   105         - new_course_data 为 None：检测该用户所有已有课程之间的冲突
   106         - new_course_data 有值：检测新课程与所有已有课程的冲突
   107 
   108         Args:
   109             db:              数据库会话
   110             user_id:         用户 ID
   111             new_course_data: 新增课程数据字典（可选）
   112 
   113         Returns:
   114             dict: {
   115                 "has_conflict": bool,
   116                 "conflicts": [{"course_a": {...}, "course_b": {...}, "reason": "..."}],
   117                 "summary": "..."
   118             }
   119         """
   120         # 从数据库查询该用户的所有课程
   121         existing_courses: List[Course] = (
   122             db.query(Course)
   123             .filter(Course.user_id == user_id)
   124             .all()
   125         )
   126         logger.info(f"用户 {user_id} 共有 {len(existing_courses)} 门课程，开始冲突检测")
   127 
   128         conflicts = []
   129 
   130         if new_course_data:
   131             # ── 场景 1：检测新课程与已有课程的冲突 ──────────────
   132             for existing in existing_courses:
   133                 conflict_reason = self._check_two_courses(new_course_data, {
   134                     "id":          existing.id,
   135                     "name":        existing.name,
   136                     "day_of_week": existing.day_of_week,
   137                     "start_time":  existing.start_time,
   138                     "end_time":    existing.end_time,
   139                     "start_week":  existing.start_week,
   140                     "end_week":    existing.end_week,
   141                 })
   142                 if conflict_reason:
   143                     conflicts.append({
   144                         "course_a": {
   145                             "id":   new_course_data.get("id", "新课程"),
   146                             "name": new_course_data.get("name", "待添加课程"),
   147                             "day_of_week": new_course_data.get("day_of_week"),
   148                             "start_time":  new_course_data.get("start_time"),
   149                             "end_time":    new_course_data.get("end_time"),
   150                         },
   151                         "course_b": {
   152                             "id":   existing.id,
   153                             "name": existing.name,
   154                             "day_of_week": existing.day_of_week,
   155                             "start_time":  existing.start_time,
   156                             "end_time":    existing.end_time,
   157                         },
   158                         "reason": conflict_reason,
   159                     })
   160         else:
   161             # ── 场景 2：检测所有已有课程两两之间的冲突 ───────────
   162             # 使用双层循环，i < j 避免重复检测（A-B 和 B-A 算同一对）
   163             for i in range(len(existing_courses)):
   164                 for j in range(i + 1, len(existing_courses)):
   165                     ca = existing_courses[i]
   166                     cb = existing_courses[j]
   167                     conflict_reason = self._check_two_courses(
   168                         {
   169                             "id": ca.id, "name": ca.name,
   170                             "day_of_week": ca.day_of_week,
   171                             "start_time": ca.start_time, "end_time": ca.end_time,
   172                             "start_week": ca.start_week, "end_week": ca.end_week,
   173                         },
   174                         {
   175                             "id": cb.id, "name": cb.name,
   176                             "day_of_week": cb.day_of_week,
   177                             "start_time": cb.start_time, "end_time": cb.end_time,
   178                             "start_week": cb.start_week, "end_week": cb.end_week,
   179                         }
   180                     )
   181                     if conflict_reason:
   182                         conflicts.append({
   183                             "course_a": {
   184                                 "id": ca.id, "name": ca.name,
   185                                 "day_of_week": ca.day_of_week,
   186                                 "start_time": ca.start_time, "end_time": ca.end_time,
   187                             },
   188                             "course_b": {
   189                                 "id": cb.id, "name": cb.name,
   190                                 "day_of_week": cb.day_of_week,
   191                                 "start_time": cb.start_time, "end_time": cb.end_time,
   192                             },
   193                             "reason": conflict_reason,
   194                         })
   195 
   196         # 生成检测摘要
   197         has_conflict = len(conflicts) > 0
   198         if has_conflict:
   199             summary = f"⚠️ 检测到 {len(conflicts)} 处课程时间冲突，请及时处理！"
   200         else:
   201             summary = "✅ 未发现课程时间冲突，课表安排合理。"
   202 
   203         logger.info(f"冲突检测完成：{summary}")
   204         return {
   205             "has_conflict": has_conflict,
   206             "conflicts":    conflicts,
   207             "summary":      summary,
   208         }
   209 
   210     def _check_two_courses(self, course_a: dict, course_b: dict) -> Optional[str]:
   211         """
   212         检查两门课程是否冲突，返回冲突原因或 None
   213 
   214         Args:
   215             course_a: 课程 A 的字典数据
   216             course_b: 课程 B 的字典数据
   217 
   218         Returns:
   219             str | None: 冲突原因描述，无冲突则返回 None
   220         """
   221         # 不同天的课程不会冲突
   222         if course_a.get("day_of_week") != course_b.get("day_of_week"):
   223             return None
   224 
   225         # 检查周次是否有交集
   226         if not self._week_overlap(
   227             course_a.get("start_week"), course_a.get("end_week"),
   228             course_b.get("start_week"), course_b.get("end_week"),
   229         ):
   230             return None  # 周次不重叠，不冲突
   231 
   232         # 检查时间是否重叠
   233         if self._time_overlap(
   234             course_a.get("start_time", ""), course_a.get("end_time", ""),
   235             course_b.get("start_time", ""), course_b.get("end_time", ""),
   236         ):
   237             day_names = ["周一", "周二", "周三", "周四", "周五", "周六", "周日"]
   238             day = day_names[course_a.get("day_of_week", 0)]
   239             return (
   240                 f"{day} {course_a.get('start_time')}~{course_a.get('end_time')} 与 "
   241                 f"{course_b.get('start_time')}~{course_b.get('end_time')} 时间段重叠"
   242             )
   243 
   244         return None
   245 
   246 
   247 # 全局冲突检测器单例
   248 conflict_detector = ConflictDetector()
   249

     1 """
     2 ============================================================
     3 学习统计报告生成服务模块
     4 从 MySQL 读取打卡记录和任务数据，生成周报/月报统计摘要
     5 ============================================================
     6 """
     7 
     8 import logging
     9 from datetime import datetime, timedelta, date
    10 from collections import defaultdict
    11 from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    12 from sqlalchemy import func
    13 
    14 from models.orm_models import Checkin, Task, TaskStatus, Goal
    15 
    16 logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17 
    18 
    19 class ReportService:
    20     """
    21     学习统计报告服务
    22     支持生成周报（最近 7 天）和月报（最近 30 天）
    23     """
    24 
    25     def generate(self, db: Session, user_id: str, period: str) -> dict:
    26         """
    27         生成学习统计报告
    28 
    29         Args:
    30             db:        数据库会话
    31             user_id:   用户 ID
    32             period:    报告周期，"week" 或 "month"
    33 
    34         Returns:
    35             dict: 包含统计数据的报告字典
    36         """
    37         # ── 计算报告时间范围 ─────────────────────────────────
    38         today = date.today()
    39         if period == "week":
    40             # 周报：最近 7 天（含今天）
    41             start_date = today - timedelta(days=6)
    42             period_label = "周报"
    43         else:
    44             # 月报：最近 30 天（含今天）
    45             start_date = today - timedelta(days=29)
    46             period_label = "月报"
    47 
    48         end_date = today
    49         start_str = start_date.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    50         end_str   = end_date.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    51 
    52         logger.info(f"生成{period_label}：用户 {user_id}，时间范围 {start_str} ~ {end_str}")
    53 
    54         # ── 统计打卡数据 ─────────────────────────────────────
    55         # 查询该时间范围内的所有打卡记录（关联目标获取课程分类信息）
    56         checkins = (
    57             db.query(Checkin)
    58             .join(Goal, Checkin.goal_id == Goal.id)
    59             .filter(
    60                 Checkin.user_id == user_id,
    61                 Checkin.checkin_date >= start_str,
    62                 Checkin.checkin_date <= end_str,
    63             )
    64             .add_columns(Goal.name.label("goal_name"), Goal.category)
    65             .all()
    66         )
    67 
    68         # 累计总打卡次数和总学习时长
    69         total_checkins = len(checkins)
    70         total_minutes  = sum(row.Checkin.duration_minutes for row in checkins)
    71 
    72         # 按目标/科目分类统计学习时长
    73         subject_map: dict[str, dict] = defaultdict(lambda: {"minutes": 0, "count": 0})
    74         for row in checkins:
    75             key = row.goal_name or row.category or "未分类"
    76             subject_map[key]["minutes"] += row.Checkin.duration_minutes
    77             subject_map[key]["count"]   += 1
    78 
    79         # 转换为列表，按学习时长降序排列（最努力的科目在前）
    80         subject_stats = [
    81             {
    82                 "course_name":    name,
    83                 "study_minutes":  data["minutes"],
    84                 "checkin_count":  data["count"],
    85             }
    86             for name, data in sorted(
    87                 subject_map.items(), key=lambda x: x[1]["minutes"], reverse=True
    88             )
    89         ]
    90 
    91         # ── 统计任务完成情况 ─────────────────────────────────
    92         # 查询截止日期在统计范围内的所有任务
    93         tasks = (
    94             db.query(Task)
    95             .filter(
    96                 Task.user_id == user_id,
    97                 Task.due_date >= datetime.combine(start_date, datetime.min.time()),
    98                 Task.due_date <= datetime.combine(end_date, datetime.max.time()),
    99             )
   100             .all()
   101         )
   102 
   103         # 已完成任务数（状态为 done）
   104         completed_tasks = sum(1 for t in tasks if t.status == TaskStatus.DONE)
   105         # 未完成任务数（pending 或 in_progress）
   106         pending_tasks   = sum(1 for t in tasks if t.status in (TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.IN_PROGRESS))
   107 
   108         # ── 计算连续打卡天数 ─────────────────────────────────
   109         streak_days = self._calc_streak(db, user_id)
   110 
   111         # ── 组装报告数据 ─────────────────────────────────────
   112         report = {
   113             "period":               period,
   114             "period_label":         period_label,
   115             "start_date":           start_str,
   116             "end_date":             end_str,
   117             "total_checkins":       total_checkins,
   118             "total_study_minutes":  total_minutes,
   119             "total_study_hours":    round(total_minutes / 60, 1),   # 换算为小时
   120             "completed_tasks":      completed_tasks,
   121             "pending_tasks":        pending_tasks,
   122             "subject_stats":        subject_stats,
   123             "streak_days":          streak_days,
   124         }
   125 
   126         logger.info(
   127             f"报告生成完成：总打卡 {total_checkins} 次，"
   128             f"总学习 {total_minutes} 分钟，完成任务 {completed_tasks} 项"
   129         )
   130         return report
   131 
   132     def _calc_streak(self, db: Session, user_id: str) -> int:
   133         """
   134         计算用户从今天向前连续打卡的天数
   135 
   136         逻辑：从今天开始，每天检查是否有打卡记录，
   137               遇到第一个没有打卡的日期就停止计数
   138 
   139         Args:
   140             db:       数据库会话
   141             user_id:  用户 ID
   142 
   143         Returns:
   144             int: 连续打卡天数（今天没打卡则为 0）
   145         """
   146         streak = 0
   147         check_date = date.today()
   148 
   149         # 最多向前检查 365 天，防止无限循环
   150         for _ in range(365):
   151             date_str = check_date.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
   152             # 查询该日期是否有打卡记录
   153             count = (
   154                 db.query(func.count(Checkin.id))
   155                 .filter(
   156                     Checkin.user_id == user_id,
   157                     Checkin.checkin_date == date_str,
   158                 )
   159                 .scalar()
   160             )
   161             if count and count > 0:
   162                 streak    += 1
   163                 check_date = check_date - timedelta(days=1)   # 继续向前检查
   164             else:
   165                 break  # 该天没有打卡记录，连续中断
   166 
   167         return streak
   168 
   169 
   170 # 全局报告服务单例
   171 report_service = ReportService()
   172

     1 """
     2 ============================================================
     3 SQLAlchemy 数据库连接与会话管理模块
     4 负责创建 MySQL 连接引擎和提供数据库会话依赖
     5 ============================================================
     6 """
     7 
     8 from sqlalchemy import create_engine
     9 from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker, declarative_base
    10 from config import settings
    11 
    12 # ── 创建 MySQL 连接引擎 ───────────────────────────────────────
    13 # pool_pre_ping=True：每次使用连接前检测连接是否存活，避免"MySQL server has gone away"错误
    14 # pool_recycle=3600：连接池中的连接每隔 1 小时重建，防止 MySQL 自动断开长时间空闲连接
    15 # pool_size=10：连接池保持 10 个常驻连接
    16 # max_overflow=20：连接池满时最多额外创建 20 个连接
    17 engine = create_engine(
    18     settings.database_url,
    19     pool_pre_ping=True,
    20     pool_recycle=3600,
    21     pool_size=10,
    22     max_overflow=20,
    23     echo=settings.debug,  # debug 模式下打印所有 SQL 语句，方便调试
    24 )
    25 
    26 # ── 创建会话工厂 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    27 # autocommit=False：需要手动提交事务，保证数据一致性
    28 # autoflush=False：不自动将内存中的修改写入数据库，由代码显式控制
    29 SessionLocal = sessionmaker(autocommit=False, autoflush=False, bind=engine)
    30 
    31 # ── 声明式基类 ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    32 # 所有 ORM 模型类都继承自 Base，SQLAlchemy 会自动识别这些类对应的数据表
    33 Base = declarative_base()
    34 
    35 
    36 def get_db():
    37     """
    38     FastAPI 依赖注入函数，提供数据库会话
    39 
    40     使用 yield 实现上下文管理：
    41     - 请求进来时创建一个新的数据库会话
    42     - 请求处理完毕后（无论成功还是异常）自动关闭会话，归还连接到连接池
    43 
    44     使用方式（在路由函数中）：
    45         @router.get("/example")
    46         def example(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    47             results = db.query(SomeModel).all()
    48             return results
    49     """
    50     db = SessionLocal()
    51     try:
    52         yield db          # 将 db 会话注入到路由函数
    53     finally:
    54         db.close()        # 请求结束后关闭会话，释放连接
    55
